**N0: Server-authoritative early settlement only after ALL living seats explicitly respond (NEW USER RULE):**
- Inspect server createNegationWindow, stored negation_window_submissions, authenticated respond / decline_response writes, advanceNegation, settleNegationWindow, and privacy projections. Preserve all living seat IDs from the immutable opening snapshot, including no-card, disconnected, Hero-provider and conversion-provider seats. Never remove no-card seats or auto-insert Pass. All viewers retain a private legal CurrentAction (Pass-only if no card).
- After successfully persisting each authenticated Negation intent OR explicit Pass, server checks the current window's stored submissions for EVERY snapshot seat. If all are present, attempt an atomic once-only settlement immediately; otherwise wait until original T0+8s. Deadline expiry alone permits treating missing seats as implicit Pass. The existing advanceNegation pre-deadline return must admit ONLY this server-proven all-submitted exception. Do not accept further conflicting submissions after settlement claim, change seat priority or reveal individual intents.
- Race/transaction coverage: last two responses in parallel, final Pass versus deadline timer, duplicate identical submissions, stale windowId, different Cloudflare instances, CAS/lease recovery, reconnect, all responding before the timer and response exactly at expiry. Winner must be deterministic by canonical seat order, not submission time; only one real card consumed and committed; loser cards remain untouched. Avoid a costly scan on every GET/poll.
- Test genuine 4/5/6/8-seat rooms: all Pass at ~2s => immediate underlying settle, no Negation card; everyone responds with 2 competing Negations at ~3s => immediate canonical winner, not first arrival; one no-card seat silent => still wait to 8s; that no-card seat explicitly Passes last => immediate close; Hero/skill-only legal Negation => not silently ignored; disconnected seat silent => wait to 8s; new counter window has fresh snapshot and same rule; stale/racing calls => exactly one settlement.
- Privacy test actual independent human tokens and spectator wire JSON: no submitted IDs/counts, card holding, implicit eligibility filtering, who Passed or intent types. Early close may reveal only that all seats are done; document this accepted aggregate timing tradeoff. Any auto-Pass/filter based on hidden Hand is prohibited.

**N1: unlock legal Negation choice/Confirm at T0.** Reproduce with a genuine human token while root card is still central. Instrument server windowId/opensAt/deadline, currentAction/revision, responsePresentationReady, presentationBusy, activeSkillSelectionBusy, consoleDecision.primary.enabled, button disabled reason, tap/HTTP time. Allow legal private Negation selection, Confirm and Pass despite visual-only busy; keep actual network busy, legal selection, one submission per window and server deadline checks. Never globally disable presentation guards for unrelated gameplay. Browser/Worker tests: 0.2s, 1s, 2s, 3.5s and last 0.5s, genuine legal/missing card, submitted lock, Pass-only player, opponent secrecy.

**N2: full proven Negation/counter seat history.** Validate exact root/counter event IDs, card IDs, actors, ordinal and causal links, and pass ALL actually committed cards to the appropriate seat journey (including Duel pre-exchange, single-target stratagems and permitted Group/target cases). In particular inspect duelNegationPublicAction.responses vs DuelCardJourney and do not use latest publicCounterRead alone as whole history. Test Duel root → winner A → counter B → counter-counter A, 4/6/8 players, multiple top/side/local Seats, early and late screenshots, authentic refresh/reconnect, no private losers, correct interaction retirement, no regression to Duel exchange.

**N3: immediate AFTER-COMMIT public presentation (never immediate private publish).** Trace BOTH close reasons (all living seats explicitly submitted; maximum 8s deadline): last stored response if any, actual server winner commit, public event received and first visible full-size CardFace. Remove extra held-root queue/read/polling delay after actual commit; preserve canonical seat priority and new counter-window responsiveness. Test early all-Pass, early competing intents, one-silent-seat expiry, early/late counter windows, refresh, privacy, card conservation and 390/440/480px real mobile screenshot/timing evidence.

**N0–N3 delivered-code test boundary:** N0–N3 were implemented as independently verified commits. Preserve concurrency, seat priority, privacy, card conservation, API, browser/Worker D1, lint/fast and exact-SHA CI/deploy evidence. The open real-phone acceptance belongs in PENDING REVIEW, **not a gate before other UX2 work**. Coding Agent owns `HANDOVER.md`; UX3 is out of scope.

## 10. PHASE 3 — Other card families: controlled UX rollout

### 10.1 Rollout authority and completed baseline — updated 2026-10-14

**Current graph-free baseline:** §§8.1–8.5 and §10.5.3 are accepted/closed; §9.5 Negation legality/early settle passed and the R2 counter-card history fix is delivered/PENDING REVIEW; §8.7 Policy B default hold passed and R1 is delivered/PENDING REVIEW. UI-1/UI-2 original checks accepted; UI-3/UI-4 delivered/PENDING REVIEW. **Proceed autonomously R3 → R4 → H1 → H2 → H3 and §10.2 family source reviews without waiting for Reviewer acceptance.**

§7 and §8.6 are history only. §9 Plan A secret 8s-maximum Negation rules stay. §10 is an approved bounded **autonomous source-review/conditional-fix sequence** after the current UX2 refinements; **no prerequisite that §8.7/§9.5 obtain human phone acceptance first**. Coding Agent owns `HANDOVER.md`.

One true server-confirmed public CardFace → **up to 4s large centre reveal subject to Policy-B committed-response interruption** → 380ms true-actor Seat/Dock park → short proven outcome, remains the default grammar for relevant card families. **Duel is an explicit family exception**: all its committed Duel + Attack cards remain in complete **seat-attached ordered histories** (§8.1), with vertical stacks below top Seats, inward horizontal rows beside left/right Seats, and a horizontal row above the local Dock for cards genuinely authored by that local player. Private Draw, target-card picker, Inspector, Judgement, Bumper Harvest and hero-skill flows retain their dedicated contracts in §5.

### 10.2 Card-family review and rollout queue

**Autonomous family review:** After current bounded R2/R3/R4 and H1/H2/H3 development tasks, inspect §10.2 families **one bounded slice at a time** without waiting for previous items' human UX acceptance. Do not reimplement accepted §10.5.3 or already delivered Dismantle/Negation. Inspect actual source, test, and log **NO CHANGE** where nothing needs fixing. Steal is a possible first new single-target review.

**For each family first inspect current ux-v2 code and reproduce a concrete behaviour, then classify it as acceptable, bug, missing UX, privacy issue or design proposal.** No observed gap means report **no change**. ### 10.3 Autonomous execution scope and technical safety boundaries

1. **Autonomous code scope:** R1, R2 and UI-4 are delivered/PENDING REVIEW; execute R3, R4, H1, H2, H3, then conditional §10.2 family review. Keep each code/validation step small; mark **PENDING REVIEW** and automatically continue. Original UI-1/UI-2 and §10.5.3/§§8.1–8.5 accepted; UX3 future-only.
2. **Asynchronous acceptance:** Open §9.5/§8.7/§10.6 human UX checks remain PENDING REVIEW, **not blockers to other approved UX2 implementation**. Preserve all accepted controls, rules, responsive behavior and privacy boundaries.
3. **Card-family boundaries:** Sequentially source-review the next not-yet-reviewed §10.2 subgroup once current bounded refinements are implemented; **no waiting for past human acceptance**. Fix only real missing approved UX, not broad speculative changes. Do not rebuild completed Dismantle/Negation/Deflection.
4. **Bounded task cadence:** Coding Agent maintains exactly one active bounded implementation task in `HANDOVER.md`, captures real focused tests and exact-SHA CI/deploy evidence, marks completion **PENDING REVIEW**, re-reads latest design and autonomously starts the next. CI never implies human UX acceptance.
5. **Preserve delivered §9 protocol except approved N0 close condition:** Private simultaneous intent, immutable 8s MAXIMUM, canonical seat-priority winner and privacy stay. §9.5 N0 permits early settlement **only after every living player explicitly Negates/Passes**, never auto-Pass based on Hand. N1–N3 fix control gating/history/post-commit UX. Do not apply 8s to other responses or implement UX3 §11. Real public winners follow §8.7 Reveal Policy B.
6. **Owner separation:** Planner/Reviewer maintains this product design and user-reviewed status; Coding Agent maintains `HANDOVER.md`, implementation, technical evidence and its PENDING REVIEW ledger. **The current user instruction explicitly authorizes continuing all approved UX2 tasks without separate Reviewer release between them.** User review notes are picked up on the next design read.

### 10.4 Invariants across the later card-family UX

- **Single event authority:** source, target, true physical card, played-as conversion, actual author and timestamp/order are server-proven; only accepted public cards enter any reveal. No private hand/response-intent leak, phantom Attack/Dodge, invented effect or HP/damage inference. Deduplicate by real event/interaction IDs across refresh, polling and viewer switch.
- **No visual regression:** never reintroduce graph/relationship connectors, a giant centre event/Negation panel, two simultaneous public presenters, persistent DECK/DISCARD piles or overlapping Hand/Skills/Guidance/timer/Confirm/Skip. Duel's complete seat-attached multi-round histories are an explicit exception, not a generic layout for all cards.
- **Game rules preserved:** authoritative deadlines, card conservation, target privacy, card selection, rescues, effect timing and causal frame remain unchanged by presentation refinements. The §9 rule adaptation is only included when separately assigned.
- **Evidence gate:** focused rule/projection/privacy tests → real authenticated 4-player and relevant 6/8-seat gameplay → browser responsive screenshots at 390/~440/480px and wide → **user visual acceptance**. Successful build/CI or mounted DOM alone is not visual acceptance. Keep normal push-to-CI-to-Cloudflare/smoke goal ≤6 minutes; heavy matrices may remain focused/opt-in but may not be reported as completed unless run.

### 10.5 Da Qiao Deflection (琉璃) — E1/E2 and §10.5.3 USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED

**USER-ACCEPTED 2026-10-14 23:23Z: E1/E2 + §10.5.3 visual corrections passed all three real-phone checks, CLOSED.** The previously reported defects and their fix below are history, not an active task. Da Qiao Deflection **gameplay already works and must not be changed**. The implemented public-card UX is: on confirmed Deflection, show the **actual cost card** with the prominent skill name **DEFLECTION / 琉璃** in the battlefield centre, then park that cost CardFace beside **Da Qiao's genuine Seat-front/current-interaction played-card area** (above her Local Dock if the viewer is Da Qiao), just as a normal public counter/reaction card parks at its real author. The newly redirected Attack target uses the same verified Attack/Dodge UX as any normal defender. Do not change the current game rule: the chosen card is **discarded as a skill cost**, not played as Attack, Dodge or Negation.

**Historical pre-E1/E2 source review (NOT an assertion about current code):**
- Server: app/api/rooms/route.ts, attack_targeted_event redirect path around lines 6028–6085, validates real card and replacement target, claims the decision, removes one physical Hand/Equipment cost, adds an ordinary grouped public discard event plus a textual Deflection notice, changes original declaration.targetId, and resumes the new target's real Dodge decision. Equipment costs may trigger equipment_lost continuations. Keep this working rule and its tests.

New target's Dodge is a separate physical response after they actually play it, never inferred at redirection.

Typed proof and card journey were delivered; preserve them. Do not force every unrelated discard into the response renderer or fabricate a physical skill CardFace.

#### 10.5.1 Public sequence on all player screens

1. **Original Attack:** Display the original committed Attack with its true physical card, original attacker and Da Qiao as initial target: **Policy-B centre reveal** (interruptible only by a newly committed real response) then parking at the **original attacker's** actual Seat/Dock, as existing Attack UX. Its true source and root event never change after Deflection.
2. **Secret selection:** While Da Qiao considers Deflection, selects a discard cost and chooses new target, keep the cost card and intended redirect target private. No provisional central card, no pre-play card movement, no public player-option leakage. Pass, invalid, stale or timed-out actions create no Deflection card.
3. **Committed Deflection central reveal (updated by §10.5.3):** After server confirmation, group the **DEFLECTION / 琉璃 skill name ABOVE the genuine discarded physical CardFace** inside a compact subtle bordered unit in the battlefield centre. Keep authentic art, suit, rank and identity; retain one short accurate main description below/near the group. Suppress the redundant upper-right EFFECT TRIGGERED toast for the *same proven activation*. No invented Skill CardFace, duplicate Attack or extra effect panel. Use §8.7 Policy B timing and nonblocking gameplay.
4. **Park at Da Qiao:** Move that SAME cost CardFace to **Da Qiao's genuine played-card/response region**, below her top-row Hero, inward from her side Seat or above her own Local Dock. **The compact skill label stays ABOVE the parked cost-card thumbnail**, preferably within the same subtle grouped frame if space permits. Never leave the label as a below-card tag hiding the picture or at another player's Seat. Retire the group after that redirected Attack/Dodge interaction. The card physically remains in server discard; no duplicate cost/Equipment or generic discard animation.
5. **Show transferred target:** Use server-proven current Attack target to update the active target emphasis and show a **brief true notice** that the original Attack was redirected from Da Qiao to [replacement]. The original attacker's Attack CardFace is **not played a second time**, and must not be placed at Da Qiao or the replacement's Seat. No connector, path graph, or fictional counter.
6. **New target responds normally:** The replacement sees their private legal CurrentAction Dodge/skill options; other viewers see only public information. If they actually play Dodge, its genuine response **may interrupt the current centre card immediately (§8.7)**; reveal the genuine Dodge at centre and park it at their own true Seat/Dock via the existing AttackDodgeCardJourney, then show confirmed Blocked result. Otherwise show **no imaginary Dodge** and show authoritative damage/other settlement at the replacement player. Original Attack source/cost/discard remain distinct proof identities.

**Special cases:** Support cost from Hand AND Equipment; 4/6/8-seat positions including Da Qiao being local; repeated fast Attacks, reload/viewer-switch and reduced motion. When Deflection redirects one Sky Piercing Halberd group target, leave remaining target branches intact. Keep Ma Chao Cavalry, Yin-Yang Swords and further equipment-lost triggers exactly as existing server rules dictate. If a later attack is independent, retire the previous Deflection visual state without a ghost card.

### 10.6 NEW user-requested mobile UX refinements — eight concrete iPhone issues (2026-10-14)

**Grounding:** The user's first iPhone screenshot shows the PRIVATE CARD INFORMATION dialog for A♥ Raining Arrows covering the battlefield, own Hand and local Play/End controls; the dialog contains text but NOT a large genuine illustrated card. The second iPhone image shows an actual committed Dodge large at centre, original Attack parked at the attacker's Seat, and an overlong DUPLICATE bottom caption such as “Test Player 3 played Dodge against Test Player 2's Attack on Test Player 3 / ATTACK BLOCKED BY DODGE”. The user also reports that Diao Chan skill start/end have no noticeable public announcement, own Hand info control is too difficult to touch, and other players' real names are not legible beside Hero identities.

**Eight approved requirements (do not infer extra rule changes):**

**U1 — “TAKE DAMAGE” reflects a real damage-accepting decline even when legal defence IS held (user revised; §10.7 R3).** For a legal Dodge/Attack/Duel or relevant Raining Arrows response where declining means accepting the damaging hit, display **TAKE DAMAGE** whether or NOT the player has a legitimate Dodge/defence provider. Do NOT change the submitted action or force extra damage: legal server effect/armor/skill prevention still applies. A capable player choosing not to defend can still use existing decline logic; never mislabel Negation Pass, optional Hero trigger decline, a general end-turn action or a case where declining does NOT imply taking a hit. Derive response semantics from authoritative CurrentAction + legal options, not from merely counting physical cards named Dodge. Existing app/page.tsx currently special-cases Raining Arrows to TAKE DAMAGE; extend only to matching real damage-response families, with no gameplay modification.

**U2 — parked public-card thumbnails are inspectable.** Tapping a **settled/parked genuinely public** mini CardFace in the battlefield, including one's own and opponents' publicly played/counter cards, opens the same compact factual Card Details preview. It should be possible even after the full centre read finishes. Never resurrect old public cards, animate a second card, change target/action selection, or expose hidden opponent Hand or unrevealed costs. A moving card may be noninteractive until parked. Preserve actor seat ownership, counter history, causal ordering and accessibility. Present real, server-proven rank/suit/name/art and canonical rules; no guessed private ID.

**U3 — Diao Chan skill activation AND outcome must have legible public notices.** Cover explicitly (a) **Lust / 离间** and (b) **Beauty Outshining the Moon / 闭月**, when each is genuinely activated. For **Lust**, show a short public skill label and accurate activation summary, including **Diao Chan as user, two truly chosen Duel participants and which one starts**, immediately after confirmed skill acceptance. When the consequent Duel settles, show one separate concise **verified** result (who lost/accepted damage and whether the consequence applies), never speculate from HP or skip a later source of prevention. Preserve Lust's real one-card cost, Equipment-loss triggers, target legality and underlying Duel/Attack exchange; skill text is NOT a fake played Duel or Attack card. For **Beauty**, show a brief activation and final public outcome “Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card” only when the optional draw really occurred; the drawn card's identity stays PRIVATE. If declined there is no false “activated/drew” notice. The current backend already adds Lust log text and Beauty generic triggered-effect notice; first inspect the authoritative event path / client display ownership and fix the missing in-battlefield presentation, avoiding duplicate generic effect toasts and repeated labels. Other players must see the same proved skill facts; do not create fabricated CardFaces.

**U4 — public action/counter explanation ABOVE its central physical CardFace.** The screenshot's public Dodge/result caption is below the centred CardFace. Instead place a compact, legible public action/result summary **above the large central physical card** (and safely below opponent Hero row), while the true card remains centre dominant. Align by the actual dynamically measured centre-card rectangle, not a hardcoded top offset; fit 390, 440, 480 CSS-px mobile and desktop with side Seats, responsive viewport height, notch/safe-area, and separate nonblocking response Guidance/controls. If vertical room is tight, shorten/reflow text rather than overlap heroes, timers, other public cards or Hand. No new 4s timer/reveal delay, no extra modal.

**U5 — stage-only illustrated Card Details (original UI-1 accepted; now simplify duplicated metadata under §10.7 R4).** When viewing an own Hand or legally public CardFace, use a compact Inspector-like **non-modal** detail panel inside the battlefield/interaction-safe zone, not the global dark .card-info-backdrop that covers Play/End, Hand and Guidance (screenshot 1). Show actual **large card illustration/CardFace** with real rank/suit/name next to or above canonical functional rules; small title e.g. CARD DETAILS. Approx width min(92vw, 340px), maximum height strictly inside current battlefield safe rectangle with internal text scrolling if needed. Preserve visible opponent Heroes, own Hand and legal controls; do NOT darken/block the entire screen or intercept outside taps. Provide a ≥44x44 close affordance, Escape/back dismissal, focus return and accessibility semantics appropriate to a **non-modal** popover/panel. Tapping another inspectable public card switches preview directly; never changes Hand selection or turn/response state. Private Hand detail remains visible ONLY to that authorized viewer. Opponent Inspector accepted in §8.5 is not to be redesigned.

**U6 — accessible own-Hand info (original UI-2 check accepted; improve attached-card discoverability under §10.7 R4).** Current app/sequence-overrides.css renders the Hand card “i” at ~16x16px only after selecting it. Replace this with an obvious **DETAILS / information button of at least 44x44 CSS px interactive hit area** in a stable selected-card action strip above Hand or at the local Dock, without consuming the cramped card face or stealing the Play/Confirm/Skip slots. One tap of a Hand card STILL selects it as currently; a distinct “Details” action opens the small §10.6 U5 preview without playing/submitting the card. Long-press-to-preview may be an OPTIONAL secondary shortcut if implemented safely, but cannot be the ONLY way to see card details or interfere with horizontal Hand scrolling. Use the same solution for iPhone touch and keyboard/focus. When no card is selected, the control is safely hidden or disabled; when selected, it remains reachable without pixel-perfect tapping.

**U7 — every opponent Hero panel must visibly show the REAL player name AS WELL AS the Hero name (original check passed; new §10.8 H2 swaps their screen positions and improves vitals/equipment).** In the screenshots top-row hero/player labels are tiny or only Hero names seem readable; source already renders player.name but CSS currently shrinks it to 6–8px in some top rows and hides it completely for side-column Seats. Retain separate visible player identity (e.g., Test Player 3) and Hero name (e.g., Zhao Yun), both legible on all 4/6/8/10-seat layouts. Prioritise readable player identity without overwriting Switch, HP, equipment icons or hero art. Use two distinct readable identity tiers; **newer §10.8 H2 is authoritative for placement**: Hero name over artwork, player name in lower name strip, HP upper-right and Equipment below player name. Use sensible wrapping/ellipsis for long Latin/CJK names, and retain the full real name for accessible Inspect. No inferred aliases, roles, hidden character data or auto-translated identities. Keep accepted §8.1 seat attachment anchors unchanged.

**U8 — shorten and deduplicate public card/action/result prose.** The second screenshot repeats DODGE, “played Dodge ... Attack on ...” and “ATTACK BLOCKED BY DODGE”. During a current public CardFace reveal use **at most ONE compact authored sentence or one result statement** plus the actual physical card; avoid simultaneous heading, duplicate long explanatory text and repeated outcome. Suggested grounded wording: “Test Player 3 dodged Test Player 2's Attack.” With a damage outcome use concise “Test Player 3 took 1 damage” ONLY after proven final damage; if blocked by armor/skill, use its truthful outcome instead. In a complex chain, prefer skill/event name as a small label + concise source/target and one verified result; full details remain in game history/inspection. Existing legal action timers, authoritative causality and §8.7 visual lifecycle must remain identical. Apply this to public Attack/Dodge, committed Negation/counters, Duel and Deflection/skill notices without deleting unique meaningful public events.

#### 10.7.2 R2 — P1: stale CausalEnvelope candidate causing intermittent Stratagem/Negation centre parking (ONE REVIEW TOPIC)

**User evidence:** An 88-second iPhone recording and UX Trace were analysed by the user and their findings were supplied in this conversation. The raw video and trace are NOT attached in this turn, so their timelines are reported findings, not footage independently replayed by Planner. Reported trace build: 946f6f697b938a87e78708e0bf5b52199b8544b5; droppedEntryCount=5681 indicates older trace entries were overwritten. Confirm the reported build versus current ux-v2 before claiming a current repro.

**Exact four-seat reproduction (reported):**
1. **10–13s:** Seat 2 Cao Cao plays **Steal** on Seat 3; root card shrinks/parks near Cao Cao.
2. **18–24s:** Seat 0 Sima Yi wins **Negation**, cancelling Steal; card parks near Sima Yi. First chain reported at 23:21:25 UTC with presentation interaction.stage=NEGATION and semantics=PROVEN.
3. **52–60s:** Cao Cao plays **Burning Bridges (Dismantle)** on Seat 3; real card shrinks but remains around table centre instead of returning to Cao Cao.
4. **61–68s:** Sima Yi's real winning Negation disappears after reveal instead of reliably parking near Seat 0.
5. **69–75s:** Seat 1 Xu Chu's subsequent real counter-Negation disappears instead of reliably parking near Seat 1. Dismantle is restored and Seat 3 discards one Attack. Authoritative gameplay and public logs reportedly complete correctly.
6. During this second chain the trace reports legitimate negate CurrentAction but presentation interaction=null and rootAction=null, with no matching public-card-journey events. First chain is PROVEN, second is not. Treat this as a **projection/ownership problem hypothesis**, not proof that CSS alone causes it.

**Source-confirmed, high-priority causal bug candidate (requires reproduction):**
- In app/api/rooms/route.ts around line 3127, resolveDeferredStratagem can write pending:null and still persist resumedEnvelope when a previous Stratagem was Negated. Some nested continuations legitimately preserve causality, so do NOT blindly null every envelope.
- Around lines 3413–3442, startNegation chooses inheritedEnvelope from explicit createdEnvelope or stored room envelope. It creates a new causalRoot when inheritedCausal is absent, **but then selects sameFrameEnvelope before inheritedEnvelope before causalRoot.envelope**. Consequently, when no valid inherited context or explicitly created envelope accompanies a NEW INDEPENDENT Stratagem, a leftover stored envelope can outrank the new causalRoot envelope. Persisted pending.causal may refer to the NEW interactionId/frameId while the stored causal envelope refers to the OLD interaction. This source hazard is real; its responsibility for the reported second-round failure remains to be proven with D1.
- The same function is used for valid Duel, Group, Judgement or Hero-trigger continuations, and may receive an explicit createdEnvelope or inheritedCausal. Correct behavior is: **a fresh independent root uses the envelope belonging to its newly created context; continuation reuse is allowed ONLY on verified interactionId, activeFrameId, frame membership and relevant causal parent/child evidence**. Do not globally replace every envelope with a new root or break legitimate nested effects.
- game/presentation-v2.ts and game/presentation-snapshot.ts intentionally require consistent PROVEN root/reaction identities. Invalid identity should NOT be replaced by fabricated public proof in the client. This can explain why one phase has genuine card/log events but no public journey; verify the exact projection rejection.

**Coding Agent: three ordered substeps for this ONE P1 defect:**

**R2-A — SERVER: prove/correct causal ownership before animation changes.**
- Build a focused genuine Worker/D1 API integration test: Seat 2 Steal → Seat 0 winning Negation → Steal cancelled → Seat 2 Dismantle → Seat 0 Negation → Seat 1 counter-Negation → Dismantle resumes → Seat 3 discards an Attack. Assert each actual authoritative game effect and card count unchanged.
- Capture the persisted rooms.pending_json and rooms.causal_envelope_json **together at second root opening and each subsequent counter**. Verify distinct interaction identity from the prior cancelled Steal, matched pending causal interactionId/frameId to envelope interactionId/activeFrameId, appropriate NEGATION stage, true source/target, correct public root and response event links and PROVEN snapshot/chain.
- Add explicit stale stored-envelope case, fresh room with no envelope, valid Duel createdEnvelope path, valid same-frame/nested/group/Judgement continuation and mismatched inherited identity. Preserve existing gameplay and privacy. Only after test failure points to an actual bug, repair startNegation selection and, if appropriate, the previous cancellation envelope lifecycle without corrupting legitimate continuations. Document before/after IDs and why a candidate was rejected.
- Safe diagnostics: pending root/event/interaction/frame IDs, envelope interaction/active/checkpoint IDs and selection origin; projection failure reason. Do not log secret Hand contents, hidden submissions or tokens. Do not claim PROVEN by merely setting a flag.

**R2-B — CLIENT: verified public CardFace source, motion and true-actor parking.**
- Trace the server public timeline to client presentationSnapshot/reactive proof to app/page.tsx singleTargetNegationPublicAction, heldPublicNegationReadAction, activePublicNegationRead, publicCardDisplayEnabled, publicCardJourneySeenEventIds and app/public-card-display.tsx SingleTarget/Negation journey, scopeKey and freshEventIds. Also inspect legacy TableResolutionSequence/static fallback. Fix only the smallest actual presentation gap after R2-A: an invalid proof must not get a fake centre-only parked card.
- Every GENUINELY committed/public card must retain unique eventId, physical card ID, actorId, root/interaction identity and ordered causal history. The second root parks near **Cao Cao Seat 2**, counter #1 near **Sima Yi Seat 0**, counter #2 near **Xu Chu Seat 1**. On top Seats cards park below the correct Hero; side Seats inward; local Dock above. Each card persists during the still-active chain and retires normally once the owning interaction genuinely ends. No discarded/losing private intent can appear.
- Existing §8.7 Policy B remains: full-size centre display normally up to 4s, interrupted ONLY by another newly server-committed PUBLIC physical response; older card starts ~380ms move to its author, while the newly committed response becomes central immediately. Never wait for old 4s queue; never reveal a private Negation intent before server settles its 8s-max/all-explicit shared window.
- Recompute targets for late Seat/Dock anchors, browser resize, viewer switches/remount/poll updates. A fallback must not silently become permanent centre parking. No duplicate card DOMs, incorrect author or premature disappearance. Reuse accepted seat geometry and preserve other card families.

**R2-C — TEST: real mobile four-viewer chain and lifecycle diagnostics.**
- Run the exact reproduction across authenticated views for Cao Cao, Sima Yi, Xu Chu and the actual target; include 390/440/480 CSS px (priority ~440) and 4/6/8-player seat geometries. Additional cases: single Negation, no winner, multi-counter, repeat actor, all-seats-early vs 8s timeout, delayed public event, poll, reconnect, viewer switch, component remount, missing/late Seat anchor and resize.
- Assert all truly public event IDs appear ONCE and are visible at their real Seat/Dock while the chain is ACTIVE. Measure browser DOM markers for eventId, actorId, role, phase and actual getBoundingClientRect; compare to the real actor anchor with a bounded explicit threshold. Assert no permanent centre thumbnail without supported reason. Distinguish ordinary retirement AFTER interaction settlement from premature removal DURING the chain.
- Instrument safe timestamps for authoritative public commit, event arrival, first centre paint, first move, final parked rect, actual target rect and legitimate retirement. Show PROVEN stage/rootAction continuity during each second-root response, not only first frame. Keep server legality, card conservation and private intentions unchanged. Capture actual before/after screenshots or short recording with exact source SHA and note which tests really ran.

#### 10.7.2.1 R2-D — globally numbered Negation chain history, preserving accepted R2 (NEW phone request, 2026-10-15)

**FINAL USER CLARIFICATION (2026-10-15; authoritative, overrides any ambiguous five-card illustrative diagram): PRESERVE the already accepted ONE-CARD-at-a-time centre reveal → THAT REAL CARD parks below/alongside ITS ACTUAL AUTHOR'S HERO Seat/Dock. The only new R2-D visible element is a small global chronology number ON EACH EXISTING CardFace.** There is **NEVER a centre row or gallery of all five cards, never a new bottom/global history rail, never simultaneously five full-size central cards, never a separate sequence panel**, and no change to the accepted R2-A/B/C actor attachment, genuine card animation or Policy-B reveal/interrupt timing. Any mockup showing five cards in one horizontal row was conceptual numbering only and is explicitly **NOT** a game layout specification.

**Concrete view progression:** #1 root Steal/Burning Bridges appears alone large in the centre, then shrinks/parks under the source Hero. When server-proven #2 Negation commits, #2 alone appears large in the centre; #1 stays at its owner's Seat. Then #2 parks under its own actor Hero, and #3 alone enters centre if subsequently committed. Repeat #4/#5 likewise; if one hero authored #2 and #4, those two physically distinct parked miniatures both stay at that hero's Seat, each with its own number. All earlier real cards remain at their actual authors' seats while the genuine interaction retains them, never moved together to centre for readability. The central public explanation is a **separate stable reading band** per §10.8.4 H4, not a card history panel.

**User review and real screenshot basis:** The user ACCEPTS **R2-A/B/C's** central-to-authentic-Seat parking and committed-card retention. But in a **single Steal / Burning Bridges → repeated Negation/counter-Negation interaction** the same seat can have two stacked purple Negation cards, and up to **five cards across separate seats** (one original Stratagem plus four Negations). From the screenshots the chronological response order **cannot be decoded from author attachment alone**. This is an independently implementable **visual chronology feature**, NOT a broken server Negation priority, NOT a reason to modify the already accepted R2 causal/parking engine, and NOT a global round/turn number.

**Required exact user-facing visual contract:**
1. **One shared interaction-local sequence**, visible **on the physical CardFace itself** regardless of where it parks: **#1 original committed ROOT Stratagem**, then **#2 first genuinely committed winning Negation**, **#3 genuine counter-Negation**, **#4 next Negation**, **#5 next counter**, etc. If one player plays multiple Negations in the SAME root chain, their two separately parked real cards visibly bear **different chain-global numbers**. Restart at **#1 only for a NEW independent committed root interaction**, never on seat changes, same player acting again, extra Negation response window, polling, or reconnect. Stable event ID → ordinal mapping persists while that interaction's public card history is visible.
2. **Badges remain legible at actual parked-card miniature size** (around 48×72 CSS px) on small iPhones: one high-contrast distinct ordinal chip near the card's **upper-right**, with a quiet solid backing and size readable without zooming (target 12–14 CSS px numeral; minimum accessible hit/inspection text), without covering the rank/suit in the upper-left, inventing/covering card identity, or overlapping a vertically stacked neighbouring card's badge. The active large reveal gets the **same ordinal** and retains it when moving/parking; don't give it two badges or restart numbering in centre. Optional tiny current-step notation in the public sentence may reinforce the order, but must never replace the card's badge.
3. **Only proven cards get a number:** Use the existing server-validated reaction chain's ordered physical event links and `response.ordinal`, require unique eventId, actual `rootEventId`, consistent interaction/frame/rootResolutionId, and exact actor/card ownership. Never number private all-seat Negation **intents, Pass, skipped/no-card seats, lost bids, virtual card effects, target-card selector steps or unconfirmed plays**. Do not assign ordinals from screenshot DOM order, seat index, Hand contents or vague text such as `Negation 3`. For a partially validated chain, fail closed: only show badges for the fully proven immutable subset; no guessed suffix.
4. **Reuse already existing data and renderer:** Source inspection 2026-10-15 shows `app/public-card-display.tsx` `singleTargetJourneyEvents()` ALREADY maps root `globalOrdinal:1` and each proof-backed response `globalOrdinal: response.ordinal+1`. `PublicJourneyCard` ALREADY has a reusable `showOrdinal` flag and `.public-card-journey-ordinal` CSS, **currently used for Duel**, but `SingleTargetCardJourney` invokes `PublicJourneyCard` **without `showOrdinal`**, and the existing aria label hardcodes `Duel event`. **This is a concrete presentation gap**. Prefer enabling/reusing that proven ordinal grammar for eligible root/counter chains (and fixing the generic accessible label) over inventing a new server counter or a separate floating number rail. Verify `app/page.tsx` public action response nodes transmit actual proven ordinal (not actor-local count) for Steal, Dismantle, Duel pre-Negation, Oath/Group/Havest scopes. Do not show a global badge on non-chain ordinary one-off Attack/Dodge unless separately approved.
5. **Collision and retention; no centralized grouping:** At 4/6/8/10 seats and 390/440/480px or short-height iPhone, retain authentic committed physical cards **separately at their actual authors' Hero Seats/Dock**. Their small numbered badges must remain distinguishable, including two cards under one Hero; stack/fan/compact/scroll **within that owner's existing seat-attached region** as needed without clipping Hero/HP/Equipment/top announcement or Local Dock. **At most the newest committed physical card is full-size in the central reveal slot at a time.** Preserve existing root and previous response parked thumbnails in situ when the next response reveals. Never create a central row/collection of all five cards, global bottom history panel, floating ordinal list, connector, second CardFace or broad table re-layout. Retire the chain normally at its legitimate completion; no leak or stale ordinal into the NEXT root.

**R2-D execution/acceptance checkpoint (one bounded Coding Agent task):** Reproduce **one root + four committed physical Negations** with an author playing more than one response on the same chain. Inspect actual `PresentationV2` reaction node ordinals, `PresentationSnapshot` and `app/page.tsx` `singleTargetNegationResponses`; assert root #1, responses #2–#5 in exact commit order. Add/enable ordinal badges in `SingleTargetCardJourney`, verify `DuelCardJourney` unchanged and accessible DOM labels distinguish `Negation chain step` from Duel. In independent 4-viewer Worker/D1 browsers, test active reveal, parked top/left/right/local Seat, nested counter, viewer switch, reconnect, polling, new independent root reset, 390×844, 390×700, 440×894, 480×900 and long 5+ chain. Assert **exactly one large centre CardFace at any instant** and all earlier real cards parked in their **individual author-attached regions**, **never** a simultaneous central multi-card lineup; assert unique badge per public physical event, exact owner/event proof, no stacking-overlap or loss of private intent anonymity. Run focused client, presentation, API and browser tests plus normal build/lint/CI; report exact SHA, evidence and limitations in Coding-Agent-owned HANDOVER, mark **R2-D PENDING REVIEW** for the user's real phone. **The accepted R2-A/B/C status must remain CLOSED** and the next reviewer acceptance item remains singular at a time.

#### 10.7.3 R3 — P2: TAKE DAMAGE is an ACTION-CONSEQUENCE label, not an inventory test

**Revised user rule:** If choosing the authorized defensive decline will knowingly accept a damage-causing hit, show **TAKE DAMAGE**, even if the player owns one or more playable Dodges, Attack-as-Dodge conversions or defence skill options. If declining does NOT mean taking damage (Negation Pass, optional trigger, benign decline, end turn, truly prevented/immune damage), use the truthful existing label. No forced HP loss, no client-side damage calculation and no gameplay-rule changes. Post-decline damage/prevention still resolves server-authoritatively.

#### 10.7.4.2 R4-G — Inspect OWN Hand outside the active turn, never authorize Play (NEW phone request 2026-10-16)

**Human acceptance: R4-F ACCEPTED/CLOSED on real iPhone, 2026-10-16.** The user confirms the inside-CardFace DETAILS location and compact illustrated card/rules preview now meet the design. Keep R4-F visually and behaviorally unchanged. **Separate new defect R4-G:** nonacting players cannot inspect THEIR OWN Hand CardFaces because the Hand can only be clicked to select an actionable card. **Requirement:** own Hand rules inspection must be available when it is another player's turn or the owner otherwise has no currently authorized action; CONFIRM / PLAY must remain HTML disabled/inert in this inspect-only context. This request does not authorize free off-turn card play and is NOT a renewed failure of R4-F.

**Actual checked code, 2026-10-16 branch ux-v2:**
- In app/page.tsx renderHandCard, maySelect is the current turn/discard OR response/rescue authorization. The physical Hand button uses disabled={!maySelect || skillModeCardDisabled || responseCardDisabled(item)} and cannot be tapped off turn. Its only onClick uses toggleHandCard, which mutates ACTUAL play/discard/skill/response card-choice state.
- The inset hand-card-details-action appears only when selectedHandDetailsCard is non-null; selectedHandDetailsCardId derives from discardSelected, active skill/response costs, Serpent, or selected. An off-turn non-actor has no selection and no DETAILS control.
- The real Local Dock Play/Confirm code already uses room.isMyTurn, currentActionOwnedByViewer and canUseAction on server-projected CurrentAction. These gates must remain. An active legitimate response (Dodge, Attack in Duel, or Negation) may occur on ANOTHER player's turn; its real server-authorized Confirm MUST remain enabled when valid. Off-turn is NOT equivalent to unauthorized.

**Design: two independent modes, never mixed:**

1. **INSPECTION ONLY:** Whenever a started-game viewer has any card legitimately present in their OWN server-projected room.myHand, they can tap it when no applicable actionable card-selection authority exists. It gains a quiet inspect-focus visual and the existing 44x44 in-card DETAILS affordance, which opens the already-accepted compact real CardFace + canonical rule description. Tapping another own card switches inspect focus; DETAILS closes via current close/Escape/return-focus behaviors. Keep Hand sideways scrolling and overlap. Use their real server-known card ID, no new public reveal or other-player Hand access.
2. **ACTION MODE:** When the viewer is genuinely server-authorized to choose a card for Play, Discard, Dodge/Negation/Duel response, rescue, triggered skill/card costs or other active action, keep existing selection flows and eligibility tests intact. Genuine off-turn response cards can be selected and submitted. A disabled game-action card may still be inspected without selecting it for an action; never call an action handler merely because its face is viewable.
3. **Separate state and semantics:** Add a local inspectOnlyHandCardId (or equivalent) distinct from selected, discardSelected, serpentSelected, activeSkillSelectionState, responseProviderId and targetIds. It MUST NOT feed action selection counts, selection validity, CurrentAction, consolePrimaryCandidates, any server-request payload or actual selected cardIds. Rendering uses action-selected card DETAILS if available; otherwise uses inspection focus only when not action-selecting. Mark inspect focus separately (for example data-card-inspect-focused=true, data-card-selected=false) and preserve honest aria-pressed=false for ACTION selection in inspect-only mode. The inspect button has a meaningful accessible label such as Inspect Attack. Do not use aria-pressed=true to falsely claim a playable selection.
4. **Absolutely no off-turn actions:** In inspect-only mode Play/Confirm never becomes actionable. Keep all current server-authoritative gates and disabled states; if a primary action is already visible, it must have native disabled=true and no submit. A low-emphasis disabled Confirm placeholder in an otherwise empty primary slot is OPTIONAL if consistent with existing waiting UI; never add an enabled fake action or a large new toolbar. No effect on End, skill, response or discard permissions. Legitimately authorized off-turn responses must retain working Confirm.
5. **Correct lifecycle/privacy:** Clear inspection focus on viewer/room switch, own-Hand card removal, and transition into a new authorized action; do NOT promote previously inspected card to selected cost automatically. An ordinary same-viewer poll with identical Hand should not collapse the preview. Close an open private preview if ownership/viewer changes or the card leaves room.myHand, without showing an old owner's Hand to a new TEST SWITCH viewer. Never reveal an opponent's hidden hand through public props, logging or reconnect. Server game rules and physical card movement stay unchanged.
6. **Preserve accepted UI:** R4-F's 44x44 inspect hit area remains INSIDE the physical CardFace; **§10.7.4.3 R4-H now governs its future visible circular `i` appearance instead of the old `DETAILS` rectangle/text**. Same nonmodal art/rules preview with no unnecessary headings and no large backdrop. Protect U13-A's newly delivered multi-discard appearance; inspection focus is NOT action selection or a second discard-selected card. Preserve other publicly inspectable parked CardFaces and equipment previews.

**ONE coding-agent task: UX2-10.7.4.2-R4-G-OFFTURN-OWN-HAND-INSPECTION-01, READY after the currently active U13-B bounded task.**

Start by reproducing an AUTHENTICATED Worker/D1 game: Player 2's turn, independent Player 1 viewer with at least 3 actual private Hand CardFaces; prove via DOM that current game-card is disabled and DETAILS inaccessible, capture currentAction authorization. Implement the narrowest app/page.tsx Hand tap/inspection-focus separation and, only as needed, final-effective app/sequence-overrides.css inspect-focused lift/DETAILS placement. Off-turn click must NEVER call toggleHandCard, setSelected/setDiscardSelected, or onAction. Protect keyboard navigation, nested button hitboxes, scroll and aria, not a long-press-only solution.

**Required real-phone verification:** At 390x844, 390x700, 440x894, 480x900 and 3/5/8 overlapping Hand sizes (also 4/6/8 players where practical): off-turn tap card A -> inspect focus, inset DETAILS; actual preview rules and real art; switch to card B, close/Escape/focus return; data-card-selected remains false and native Play/Confirm cannot be clicked / makes zero action API calls. A newly authorized response on another player's turn (Attack->Dodge, or private shared Negation/real Duel) still permits genuinely legal card choice and response Confirm, and never leaks losing Negation intentions. Test a normal owner's Play and 2-card Discard/Serpent skill cost; off-turn inspect cannot contaminate subsequent selected cardIds. Recheck view SWITCH/room change/card stolen/used, polling with unchanged Hand, inaccessible other-player Hand, focus and touch pan. Real before/after DOMRect and aria/disabled assertions, focused tests + lint/build + exact-SHA CI/deploy; only after actual technical delivery mark R4-G PENDING REVIEW in HANDOVER.md, NEVER user accepted by CI.

**Human re-test:** While another player acts, inspect any own card's rule text without altering game state; CONFIRM/PLAY is disabled/inert. On a legitimate authorized response during their turn, response Confirm still works. Existing R4-F card-details UI remains accepted and need not be re-reviewed.

When the viewer taps a physical Hand CardFace and it **lifts/receives the existing inspect focus**, show **one circular info button** centred horizontally near the **BOTTOM INTERIOR** of that same physical card. Test circular button tap vs genuine card tap vs Play/Confirm, private Card Details opened/closed, button focus/VoiceOver and no false action, no overlap with bottom Hand rail/controls. Run focused UI checks, applicable lint/build and verified exact-SHA CI/deploy per current Coding Agent workflow. #### 10.7.4.4 R4-I — remove redundant Equipment miniature i; repeat-tap closes inspected Card/Hero (USER-APPROVED DESIGN 2026-10-17; NOT CODED)

**Phone feedback (IMG_3266):** Four local Equipment thumbnails show tiny corner `i` info icons although the thumbnails are directly tappable. The user explicitly wants **NO independent Equipment mini i**; in normal inspection, tap the card itself to open details, then **tap the SAME inspected card or Hero again to close** that same view; different item switches subject. Preserve existing nonmodal previews and visible ×/Escape closing. This is an OPEN/CLOSE change, not a gameplay action.

**Required behavior and conflict boundary:**
1. Normal local equipped mini card: entire physical-thumbnail button opens accepted CardDetailsPreview; remove the redundant painted and focusable `.zone-info-button` from **local Equipment slots** only (do not accidentally remove Judgement/Inspector controls requiring distinct action). Same authentic card ID tapped twice -> Open then Close, other card ID -> switch one existing preview. Never mount two overlapping dialogs, duplicate content or remove real rank/suit/art/accessibility.
2. Own Hand card Details affordance (currently `DETAILS`, future approved R4-H circular inset `i`) also toggles the SAME card's CardDetailsPreview. Do not conflate tapping the Hand CardFace (legitimate lift/selection) with its separate Details affordance. Card-detail clicks cannot Play, Confirm, discard, change cost IDs, deselect cards or break accepted simultaneous multi-selection.
3. Own Hero information opener already has toggle logic in `openHeroDetails`; retain it. Opponent Inspect opener: same opponent A -> open then close; tapping B while A is open switches to B. In target-selection mode, main portrait tap is for valid target selection; a distinct unobtrusive Inspector affordance (H2-G1-F) toggles Inspect WITHOUT targeting, and must not resurrect the rejected giant portrait circle. Confirm/tap selection and Inspect have distinct hit maps.
4. **Selection-mode exception:** when a local Equipment miniature is selectable as a Hero-skill cost, its primary tap MUST remain authoritative Select/Deselect payment, not suddenly Inspect. Maintain a separate accessible non-gameplay inspect path as needed, without a painted tiny corner i. No Inspect action may POST/trigger skill or submit a cost; no cost action may accidentally close an unrelated preview.
5. **Existing ACCEPTED `8.5 still applies to nested detail content INSIDE an already-open opponent Inspector:** same skill/equipment item tapped again should not blank the Inspector's details body; different nested items switch the content. The new repeat-tap CLOSE refers to the outer top-level CardDetails/HeroDetails/Inspector view and its original opener, not to deleting the `8.5 nested detail region. Do not silently reverse prior acceptance without explicit user instruction.
6. Keep accessible full name, focus restoration, keyboard/VoiceOver, ×, Escape, card context/privacy, viewer switch/room change invalidation, no stale cards after equipment replacement.

**Current code entrypoints:** `app/page.tsx LocalPlayerDock.renderZoneCard` uses one full-card `.local-zone-card-button` plus an overlapping `.zone-info-button`; late `app/sequence-overrides.css` creates the tiny i via `::after`. `openCardDetails` unconditionally opens (add same-card identity toggle), `openHeroDetails` already toggles by ID, opponent `onInspect` currently sets `expandedOpponentId` unconditionally. Keep public Inspector `8.5 nested item switching.

Ensure no Equipment mini corner i, unchanged Judgement where independently required, correct focus/ARIA, no duplicated panel or leaked private information. #### 10.8.1 H1 — own in-game Hero info uses a compact nonmodal battlefield panel with genuine large Hero art

**Current user-observed defect:** Tapping one's own in-game Hero opens a globally covering dark backdrop/window, unlike the recently accepted in-battlefield Card Details preview. This obscures play and response controls.

**Actual code scope:** In app/page.tsx the local Hero calls onHeroInfo(hero), controlled by infoHero. The shared HeroInfoDialog currently renders .card-info-backdrop with aria-modal=true and a text-heavy hero-info-dialog; it is also used during the *pre-game* hero selection screen. In-game CardDetailsPreview, by contrast, already renders a battlefield-only .card-details-stage.

**Final desired UI:**
- **Only during an active game**, opening one's own Hero information displays a compact, NONMODAL panel bounded by the battlefield/interaction-safe zone, visually from the same family as the accepted Card Details panel. No full-screen dark backdrop and no overlay across local Hand, response Guidance, Confirm, TAKE DAMAGE, Pass, Play or End. Do not intercept pointer input outside the panel.
- Show a **genuine readable illustrated Hero portrait** from existing HeroPortrait/art source (not placeholder or invented art), Hero canonical name, plus **every real named Hero skill and its full functional description**. **USER H1-F override (§10.8.1.1, 2026-10-15): omit redundant visible faction, HP and decorative HERO DETAILS title in the panel; HP is already on the actual local Hero Dock.** For long descriptions, scroll ONLY the skill-rules region and keep portrait/close identifiable. Do not unnecessarily duplicate the Hero name if already clearly printed on reused illustration.
- Panel width should follow the existing compact preview (approximately min(92vw, 340px)), remain INSIDE battlefield safe bounds, with minimum 44x44 px close hit area, Escape/outside-tap dismissal and focus return to own Hero. Allow switching between Card Details and own Hero Details without two stacking windows or losing the selected Hand card.
- Preserve local Hero-as-self-target tap behavior when the server has an actual self-targeting CurrentAction. A separate accessible Hero Details control must remain available in that mode; do not turn a legal target-selection tap into a preview. Never create a new gameplay action or alter private role visibility. Match iPhone 390x844, ~440x894, 480x900 and desktop / reduced-motion.

**Implementation checkpoint H1:** Extract/reuse a stage-bound HeroDetailsPreview layout in its own small component or safely reuse CardDetailsPreview shell. Update only the in-game infoHero render path; **do not accidentally change the pre-game HeroSelection's HeroInfoDialog**. Verify genuine art and all skills, scroll/close/focus, visible unblocked controls, private content confinement, active turn vs response vs self-target and no changes to opponent Inspector. Test authenticated real browser at mobile breakpoints, accessibility keyboard/touch and multiple Hero skill lengths.

H1-F is a new READY TO FIX correction**, rather than a request to redo the correctly implemented hero-info opener, gameplay, skills, or accepted nonmodal boundary. Separate R4-F Hand Card Details compacting has already been requested (§10.7.4.1), but the hero layout remains an independently bounded task.

**Final visual contract (explicit 2026-10-15 user direction supersedes H1's previous HP/faction/header display requirement):**

1. **Only true useful content remains visible:** show a genuine readable illustrated Hero portrait, **canonical Hero name**, **ALL real named skills**, and their **complete actual functional descriptions**. The user specifically wants **no displayed `HP X/Y`** in this details window (real HP stays visible in the permanent local Hero Dock / true server state). Remove visible decorative `HERO DETAILS` heading and redundant `SHU/WEI/WU/QUN` faction label when its identity is already conveyed by the hero portrait/name; do not add a new subtitle/empty label to replace them. The accessible dialog name may continue to be `<Hero> details` via semantic label; removing decorative text must not remove legitimate skill facts, privacy protection, keyboard focus semantics, or any real HP from the game itself. Preserve full names if portrait cropping truncates the hero label.
2. **Content-height, not fixed-height:** the entire panel is **just tall enough** for the compact Hero profile + exact skill sections + small inner margins, rather than always `height:min(440px,100%)`. There must be **no large unused blank region below the last short skill**. Keep sensible `max-height` based on the **actual safe battlefield corridor** (`card-details-stage`, opponent seat row and Local Dock). For unusually many/long skills, cap inside this safe region and allow ONLY the skill-description section to scroll; never enlarge the whole stage across Hand, Guidance, responses or browser chrome. A Hero with exactly two short skills (e.g. screenshot Huang Yueying) should visibly fit as a **small content-dense panel**; a Hero with many long skill descriptions may be taller but never clips hidden skills or close action. Do not hardcode one pixel height that fits only this screenshot.
3. **Layout composition and sizing:** use a compact 2-column top section (portrait approximately 72–82px wide at 390–480px, genuine aspect ratio) and hero name to its right. Place the **existing close × hit target (minimum 44×44 CSS px)** unobtrusively at the top-right of the same profile row (or top-right inset), **not** a separate 44px-tall decorative header row. Skill list goes immediately below with modest vertical spacing, subtle separators only if necessary, readable 13–14px description/body and distinguishable named headings. Reduce unnecessary panel padding/gaps; maintain consistent style with the separately requested R4-F compact Card Details. No giant backdrop, modal capture, second pop-up, large blank padding, or translucent ghosting of game-over/status text through the body.
4. **Privacy/behavior untouched:** only during an active game, the own-Hero detail panel stays a **nonmodal, battlefield-contained** preview. Underlying Hand, target/response timer, Guidance, Confirm/Pass/Play/End remain visible and usable; outside tap/Escape/close works, with focus return to actual opener. Self-target CurrentAction must still allow legal self-target selection, with a separate accessible way to open the info; pre-game Hero picker `HeroInfoDialog`, opponent Inspector (§8.5), the real local Dock HP and complete skill rule text must not be changed. Responsive mobile 390×844, 390×700, 440×894, 480×900, long translations/names, 1/2/3+ real skills, 4/6/8 seats, scrolled states and match-complete must all be safe.

**Source-confirmed cause to test rather than guess:** `app/page.tsx` `HeroDetailsPreview` currently emits `.hero-details-heading` with visible `HERO DETAILS` and ×, `<span>{hero.faction}</span>` and `<strong>HP ...</strong>`. `app/sequence-overrides.css` effective `.hero-details-panel` uses **`height:min(440px,100%)`**, `gap:10px`, `padding:12px`, and a separate `min-height:44px` `.hero-details-heading`, which jointly explain unused space. **Change the smallest relevant JSX/CSS only**: actual content-based height, bounded max-height, close button in compact profile, suppress redundant visible labels. Verify final CSS precedence at mobile breakpoints, including `@media(max-height:560px)` which currently sets `height:100%`; don't accidentally reintroduce a full-height panel on short phones.

#### 10.8.3 H3 — concise PUBLIC battle announcements with larger, wider, easier-to-read type

**User issue and requested emphasis:** The public battlefield explanation (e.g. Player A uses a card) should be **much easier to read**: larger font, visually wider characters (not compressed/ultra-condensed) and SHORTER truthful content. This builds on delivered UI-3, which already moved the event notice ABOVE the physical centre CardFace and removed redundant result text.

**Final visual contract:**
- During the current card's **large REVEAL**, its factual public event message remains **ABOVE that actual committed physical CardFace**; during card move/park, the message's **independent fixed-stage placement is specified solely by §10.8.4 H4-A/B/C**, not H3. The public announcement is ideally one clear sentence; prefer **14–16px medium/bold readable sans-serif on 390–480px mobile** and about 16px on desktop. Use a normal/wide font cut with natural letter spacing, roughly 0–0.02em (no narrow/condensed text and no excessive all-caps tracking). High text/background contrast; widen the actual text container as needed up to the safe battlefield width, rather than making the font tiny to fit a narrow 320–360px box. Aim one line for short events and **at most two legible lines** for normal actions.
- The wording should identify **real actor + card/skill + immediate verified target/result when meaningful**, in plain short English following the current game's language. Examples (only if proven by public event): "Player A played Attack on B."; "B played Dodge."; "C's Negation cancelled the Stratagem." Avoid replaying the CardFace title in a second huge heading, repeated Attack/Dodge category badges plus a long redundant explanation, and unsupported claims about damage before settlement.
- Reuse **the single already-existing public CardFace event and authoritative text**; shorten client presentation copy only, preserving event ordering, true actor/target, skill identity, private Hand privacy, all important outcome meaning and accessible chronological log. Keep existing UI-3 positioning and §8.7 4s/interruption; DO NOT add a second toast, pause the next legal response, change game rules, obscure the opponent Hero row/HP, or expand a full-screen dialog.
- Responsive positioning MUST work with H2's taller/larger opponent name and Equipment row; measure true centre CardFace and usable battlefield region, avoid clipping/overlap with side Seats, HP badges, equipment, Guidance and bottom Hand on short iPhones. If copy cannot fit in two lines, rewrite it to a SHORTER factual sentence rather than truncate a meaningful outcome. Keep full information in existing history/Inspector surfaces.

**Actual code anchors:** app/globals.css currently sizes .public-card-display-journey-copy > b at 10px (9px condensed), and app/public-card-display.tsx owns the action-specific proof-backed event copy/geometry. Fix the computed final CSS and only the proved presentational summaries; do not enlarge all HTML body text or outdated legacy panels. The Deflection framed skill/cost arrangement and already-accepted omission of its duplicate EFFECT TRIGGERED toast remain untouched.

**Implementation checkpoint H3:** Typography/geometry and concise-authoritative-copy tests for Attack, Dodge, Negation, counter, Duel, Deflection, Dismantle, group card and Diao Chan skill notices where already delivered. Validate width/font CSS properties and actual screenshot at 390/440/480px and short-height variant, with long player names and 6/8 Side Seats, multiple consecutive committed cards, faster legal responses, reduced motion, and no duplicate text or privacy loss. #### 10.8.4 H4 — unified stage-fixed and stable public announcement: horizontal alignment, vertical placement, motion decoupling and no deformation (iPhone reports, 2026-10-15)

**Source of this requirement / review boundary:** The user explicitly **ACCEPTED R1 (§10.7.1)** after real iPhone testing: while a prior Attack is present, its defender can commit Dodge, the new genuine Dodge occupies the centre and the previous Attack is parked at the earlier player's actual Seat. The same user-provided screenshot (2026-10-15, approximately 10:15 UK local on iPhone Safari) reports **a separate layout defect**: the public battle announcement reading `Test Player 2 played Dodge against Test Player 3's Attack.` appears as a wide black strip shifted notably **to the RIGHT** of the large centrally revealed **Dodge** card. The parked earlier Attack thumbnail occupies the left of the battlefield, adjacent to the announcement. **Do not reopen R1 response authorization, its immediate response timing or the accepted card author-parking behavior.** This new task is **H4 announcement centering**, not R1 gameplay regression and not a request to restore central relationship arrows.

**Observed vs expected composition:** The **large authentic currently committed Dodge** and its short public explanation are **visually aligned at centre during REVEAL, while the banner is an independently positioned stage narration layer during the genuine card's MOVE/PARK lifecycle (H4-C)**. The existing design correctly puts the announcement **ABOVE** its associated real card (§10.8.3 H3), and the old Attack remains at its correct author Seat. But the message box centre must be anchored to the *actual active centre CardFace midpoint* rather than allowed to drift toward the upper-right just to avoid the parked thumbnail. On a normal 390–480px phone target **horizontal centre difference ≤12 CSS px** between announcement box and live centre CardFace; aim ≤8px. No cap on a simple always-centre design is required if justified by true safe-area/obstacle geometry, but an **80px-style drift like the provided screenshot fails**. Keep the announcement readable, not unnaturally tiny or compressed, and at most **two normal-width lines** for ordinary Attack/Dodge copy where possible.

**Source-grounded likely cause to verify, not blindly patch:** `app/public-card-display.tsx` `PublicCardAnnouncement.place()` currently enumerates candidate widths **largest first** (up to 520px), then tests shifted left offsets up to ±96px to avoid `.opponent-player-card`, `.player-square`, `[data-public-card-copy-anchor-event-id]` and other obstacles. Because large-first fitting outranks symmetry, a parked earlier Attack can induce a very wide banner **shifted right**, even though a **narrower symmetric** box or shorter factual sentence would fit above Dodge. The existing `compactMessage` option is only tried **after all original-message widths and shifts**; prefer it early when central alignment otherwise fails. There may also be stale/moving anchor geometry during Policy-B centre→Seat transitions. **Measure real DOM before choosing the fix.** `app/globals.css` sets `.public-card-display-journey-copy` positioning/text; `app/sequence-overrides.css` and `app/page.tsx` contain responsive seat/card geometry. Check final computed CSS (including mobile overrides) rather than one intermediate stylesheet declaration.

**Normative design / algorithm constraints:**
1. **Anchor to the active real CardFace:** Every per-event public announcement derives its horizontal centre from the genuine active **revealed** card's live measured rectangle. During the transition to park, use the last measured **centre-reveal** rect or the authoritative battlefield-centre fallback, **not** the moving/parked thumbnail's location. A new accepted Dodge supersedes the previous Attack as active public explanation; the announcement must follow **Dodge**, not the older Attack card, or become attached to an unrelated Seat/status.
2. **Centered candidate wins over width:** Try **centered** widths first (largest width that fits in the centered safe corridor with ≤2 lines), then switch to a **shorter factual `compactMessage`** if needed; consider a small **vertical adjustment** within genuine battlefield safe bounds rather than sliding a wide banner sideways. Selection objective should first enforce valid non-overlap, safe area and accessibility, then **minimize absolute horizontal centre offset**, then prefer readability and useful width. Do **not** retain the current width-first, 96px lateral-offset outcome when a narrower centered candidate exists. Avoid shift flicker when resizing or parked cards animate.
3. **Friendly readable copy:** The event can be summarized concisely as `Test Player 2 played Dodge vs Test Player 3.` **only when server-proven** (names, actor, target and response type). Do not remove meaning, infer damage/outcome, duplicate the card title as a giant additional banner, or change the already delivered H3 font rules. Use normal/wide ~14–16px mobile type, at most two useful lines; adapt banner width to available centre corridor. If unavoidable collisions leave no valid centered two-line location, use a verified brief plain sentence and minimum **bounded** deviation, not clipping/overlapping the top Hero row, parked Attack, side Seats, Guidance or Hand.
4. **Safe placement around real cards:** Preserve previous Attack thumbnail and true Seat/Dock history. Do **not** move the root Attack to make space, shrink the active Dodge CardFace, hide live player/HP/Equipment labels, enlarge the stage or overlay side Seats. The banner is a noninteractive status and may not intercept pointer/touch/focus; preserve the active response clock and any immediate next legal response. **During full-size REVEAL**, the announcement sits just **above** the actual central physical card with a narrow gap. **During MOVE/PARK, it stays at that independent canonical central stage band** and never follows the thumbnail; it is not docked at viewport top/right. See latest H4-C.
5. **No duplicates, wrong card binding or layout flash:** Position exactly one announcement for the newest real public event, with its own correct eventId, source and target. Test root Attack→Dodge, Attack→multiple Dodges, Duel Attack and response, Negation/counter, later root Stratagem with parked old card, and Diao Chan/Deflection public cases where different layout semantics apply. Frequent polling, reflow, iPhone Safari address-bar changes, rotated viewport, reconnect, new response interrupt and reduced-motion settings may remeasure position but must not move the banner unexpectedly to the wrong Seat or create second public text.
