#### 10.13.7 P1-G — Compact opponent Inspector: two judgement placeholders + four equipment slots, aligned in one row

This is a **specific new user-requested layout refinement** to §8.5's already accepted inspector semantics; do not reopen its skill/card details behavior, keyboard switching, public/private boundary, card inspector semantics or nested Details flow.

Current OpponentPublicInspector in app/page.tsx shows Equipment then Judgement; mobile effective .hero-focus-inspect-public-zones in app/sequence-overrides.css changes to one column. Replace the zones section with a **single compact row of two side-by-side groups, explicit order**:

~~~text
┌─────────────────────────── OPPONENT INSPECT ──────────────────────────┐
│ Portrait  Hero / HP / public Hand  | Public skills                    │
│  JUDGEMENT                         | EQUIPMENT                        │
│  [delayed 1] [delayed 2]          | [sword] [shield] [+1] [−1]        │
│  (same band height; vacant boxes retain neutral outline)             │
│  Expanded public skill / actual card details (only when selected)     │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
~~~

- **Exactly two visually reserved Judgement positions** and **four equipment positions**, filled from genuine projected public cards; derive equipment into weapon / armor / +1 / −1 slots instead of arbitrary array ordering. Actual Judgement may legally exceed two: allow a concise “+N” expandable/scrollable overflow or accessible detail list without silently hiding cards; no artificial game rule cap.
- Use 2-column **outer** grid with about 1:2 width ratio (two cards vs four), no 1-column media fallback on 390px phone. Inside: grid 2 and grid 4, identical slot aspect ratio/heights, same heading spacing, min-width:0 and no horizontal viewport overflow; vacant groups show outlined blanks rather than “None” text that changes heights. Section band auto-sizes to one row and stays equal height. Portrait/profile/skill details stay readable, card/skill inspection switches instantly.
- Smaller content-height panel with height based on content; details area scrolls **internally** when expanded, not the entire page. Preserve top screenshot no-overlap requirement by moving the floating inspector **below the bottom edge of the top opponents + >=12px clearance**, while clamping its bottom to the battlefield/safe-zone above Local Dock/Hand. Geometry should use actual measured seat and visualViewport rects, not a hard-coded translateY which fails for 4/6/8/10-seat top/side topology. When insufficient room, reduce portrait/padding or internally scroll details, never cover clickable top heroes or Local Hand. Set a coherent close-button, backdrop and focus behavior. No excessive empty bottom section after compacting.
- Reuse real public equipment cards for inside inspector when users tap the four slots (unlike miniature seat icons), with original rank/suit/Details and physical-card semantics.
- Phone targets: top Heroes remain visibly distinguishable/targetable when inspector open; panel under them and above Local Dock at 390×700, 390×844, 440×894, 480×900; correct 2+4 cells even when 0/1/4 Equipment, 0/1/2/3+ Judgement, long/Chinese names, large descriptions and public card-detail expansion.

### 10.14 UI-4B-F — Diao Chan Beauty Outshining the Moon: public DRAW-1 result and stage-centered notice (2026-10-15 real iPhone)

**Human real-phone decision: UI-4B BEAUTY NOT ACCEPTED; focused corrective work is APPROVED and READY for Coding Agent.** Screenshot IMG_3186.jpeg at 23:55 from Diao Chan's own iPhone: the battlefield shows a top-right floating `BEAUTY / Diao Chan activated Beauty Outshining the Moon.` notification over the upper-player/right battlefield space, while a genuine PRIVATE DRAW panel displays an actual Q♥ Peach to its owner. The bottom independent public activity chip correctly identifies a current turn, but the public skill notification never clearly describes the key result **“drew 1 card” during the visible draw**. User requires proper **stage-central public information placement** and **one truthful explicit public draw outcome**. Do not imply other viewers were shown Q♥; the screenshot comes from the rightful viewer. This is a real UX defect separate from the original Lust correction and the already accepted H3 typography; keep Beauty human acceptance OPEN.

#### 10.14.1 Source proof already exists; current CLIENT sequencing and CSS explain likely screenshot

**Actual code read at latest ux-v2:**
- **Server is already authoritative.** `app/api/rooms/route.ts` Beauty branch after accepted optional `apply_trigger`: invokes `drawCards(...,1,...)`, updates only Diao Chan's actual private Hand, emits one public `publicDiaoChanNotice` `ACTIVATION` with `sourceId` and, **iff exactly one card was actually drawn**, another public `DRAWN` with `rootEventId` pointing to activation, the same `sourceId`, `drawCount:1`, `finalResult:true`; adds separate PRIVATE physical draw event for the actual drawn CardFace. Do not replace/forge the public proof using private card ID, optimistic counter or animation.
- **Projection validates causality.** `game/presentation-v2.ts` `diaoChanSkillNoticesFor()` strictly checks that a Beauty DRAWN notice points to one actual matching Beauty ACTIVATION of the same Diao Chan, with genuine `finalResult`, `drawCount===1`; the public field contains the count but **not the drawn card face**.
- **Correct public English wording ALREADY EXISTS.** `app/page.tsx diaoChanNoticeCopy()` formats `ACTIVATION` as `BEAUTY / Diao Chan activated Beauty Outshining the Moon.` and `DRAWN` as `BEAUTY RESULT / Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card.`
- **Displayed result can lag behind activation.** In `app/page.tsx`, `queuedNotices` iterates public message events and appends each separate proven Beauty event to `effectNotices`. The visible `effectNotice` is the **queue's head**, with a `UI_TIMING.effectNotice` expiry before the following notice is shown. Even when server commits ACTIVATE and DRAWN into the same updated timeline, the iPhone can still see activation-only while the result remains queued. Verify with event ID/head/queue traces; do not assume the second event was missing or delayed on the server. The screenshot is consistent with head-of-queue presentation lag, not evidence that the draw failed.
- **Right-floating CSS is DEFINITE.** The current `.game-shell .play-table > .interaction-safe-zone > .effect-notice` in `app/globals.css` is `top:8px;right:clamp(...)`, `width:min(320px,...)`, causing the exact wrong-side caption. Only Lust COST/Duel notice IDs currently receive special suppression via `publicCardDisplayAction`; Beauty still goes through the independent top-right generic notice. Preserve other proven effect notices when redirecting this skill.

**UI semantics / expected public copy:**
- **Confirmed exactly-one draw (server `DRAWN` proof):** one visible notice `BEAUTY` with main factual copy **`Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card.`** (optionally `Diao Chan · Beauty — drew 1 card` as compact wording). Actor name must come from verified public `sourceId`; do not hard-code Diao Chan's player label if a named player should be shown, and NEVER display `Peach`, `Q♥`, physical ID, suit, rank, Hand index or an opponent's private new card.
- **Committed activation but no linked DRAWN yet:** a short provisional **`Diao Chan activated Beauty`** is allowed only while proof is genuinely pending. Do **not** show `drew 1 card` until linked `DRAWN` exists. If ACTIVATE+DRAWN are present together, **prefer the complete final DRAWN statement immediately**; do not force users to wait through a 2.4s activation toast. If only ACTIVATE appears and DRAWN is later proven, REPLACE/UPGRADE the original text IN PLACE rather than queueing an unrelated second toast.
- **Declined, stale, unsuccessful (zero-card) draw, or unrelated Hero event:** no invented draw result. A declined optional Beauty action should not emit a false activation/success notice; keep other Hero notifications intact.
- **No duplicate noisy labels:** one coherent public skill-event announcement, NOT `BEAUTY` + `BEAUTY RESULT` consecutively with the same two messages. Use a small skill title only as context; present the genuine draw as the main sentence. For screen readers, announce the final proof exactly once and avoid a stale provisional announcement reappearing after polling.

#### 10.14.2 One stage-centered public-event narration layer (coordinate with current H4) and independent PRIVATE DRAW

**Approved placement:** Beauty's PUBLIC status is a stage-wide PUBLIC EVENT, not a physical card, because the skill has **NO publicly committed cost CardFace**. Reuse **H4's canonical centred public-event narration geometry and visual ornament** as a common positioning owner for both genuine card events and proven no-physical-card Hero skill events. Do NOT create a phantom central Beauty CardFace, a top-right `.effect-notice`, a second banner, a standalone huge skill window, or a public picture of drawn Hand. Stage-center alignment should be **≤12 CSS px** from the canonical reading-band center and stable top within **≤16px for unchanged geometry**, consistent with H4's measured safe corridor.

**Crucial private-draw concurrency:** Unlike Attack→Dodge, the Beauty notice can coexist with a real `.private-draw-stage` and its `PRIVATE DRAW / ONLY YOU CAN SEE THESE CARDS` label+card. Reserve the actual PRIVATE DRAW visual rectangle as a protected collider **on that same local viewer**; the public notice sits in the NEAREST stable central battlefield reading band **above the private draw**, with a clear **8–12px gap**, below top Hero/Equipment/Seat/parked physical public history. If the regular H4 card-above position is blocked by the enlarged private draw on a 390×700 viewport, allow a narrowly tested alternative centre sub-band, NOT right docking and NOT overlaying the top Hero. On non-owner views with NO private draw, the public caption uses the same canonical central band. It must not jump sideways when the private drawn card appears/vanishes, follow that card or cover the bottom public activity chip/timer/Local Guidance/Hand.

The `publicDiaoChanNotice DRAWN` carries no physical CardFace. Test that public-room snapshot/timeline, rendered DOM, accessible name, client payload and replay/event cache NEVER contain the drawn card ID/face for other users. Do not use `<CardFace>` in a public Beauty caption; keep the old private draw reveal/timer behaviour and server Hand conservation exactly as-is.

It must use the same measured reading-band geometry and collision owner as H4, without making fake `publicCardDisplayAction.cardFace`. Only one active public EVENT caption may own the reading band at a time; newest proven event supersedes older same-skill activation, and a genuinely newer Attack/Negation/public card can supersede the old Beauty event according to the existing policy. Use no added reveal delay and do not block response controls or timers.

#### 10.15.5 Binding implementation contract — physical lifecycle, semantic checkpoint and viewer geometry are separate clocks

**Single-owner rule:** For one validated (roomCode, interactionId, rootFrameId, rootEventId, physicalCardId, actualAuthorId), only one **physical root presentation coordinator** may render the public root. It must persist across NEGATION, GROUP_RESOLUTION participant 1/2/3, damage, optional triggered skill and settlement, even when presentationRevision, checkpointId, participant, response timer, selection branch or notice changes. Build this linkage from PROVEN server/public relationships; never join by latest timeline item, card kind, player name or turnSeat. If evidence of the linkage is missing, fail closed rather than guess. Other unrelated card families may still use their generic fallback; a root already owned/parked by a Journey must **never** return through static fallback.

| Independent clock | Stable authority | Events allowed to change it | Forbidden side effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Physical event identity** | Proven root event + physical card + true author + typed interaction/frame proof | A different genuinely committed physical card | Duplicate or impersonated root |
| **Visual lifecycle** | Same physical event state: UNSEEN → REVEAL → MOVE → PARKED → RETIRED | First public commitment, normal ≈4s reveal, ≈380ms move, real interrupt or retirement | PARKED→REVEAL from a new checkpoint, response, poll or switching viewers |
| **Semantic checkpoint** | PROVEN interaction/decision/current participant/settlement | Server advances group target, Negation, damage, outcome | Restart visual root or move already parked root |
| **Viewer layout epoch** | Viewer ID + measured Seat/Dock/topology + visualViewport | TEST SWITCH, reconnect, resize/rotation, topology changes | Old-view-to-new-view flying animation; wrong author |
| **Public narration** | Latest proven public stage/event copy via H4 | Genuine server-proven semantic stage change | Text follows shrinking root, stale previous-stage notice, false private reveal |

**Q2 / state ownership:** extend one typed Group-root Journey throughout normal Group response and GROUP_RESOLVED, including phases where Negation is not active. Preserve existing exact source/root/card/frame proof, root actor identity, historical genuine Negations and distinct physical response cards. A genuinely NEW committed response card may reveal at centre and interrupt the root; the old root remains PARKED. The old root must not be reselected as a new central event by an unrelated target response or settlement result. Implement arbitration once at a shared presenter boundary, not separately per group stage. Do not merely hide CSS static cards while still mounting duplicate physical owners.

**Q3 / first-paint view barrier:** visual phase/commit history belongs to the root, while layout coordinates belong to the CURRENT viewer. On TEST SWITCH or legitimate layout-epoch change, reproject already PARKED roots to the new measured true-author anchor BEFORE first paint, with transition disabled. Do not animate from cached actualCardRect belonging to another viewer, do not flash at stale coordinates and do not replay 4s because an event is new to the observer. If new anchor is unmeasurable, postpone that card's painting rather than use 0,0 or guess. A newly confirmed physical card in the same, unchanged view still gets the accepted 4s/380ms motion. Test on Attack/Dodge, Duel/Lust and other Journey types too, while keeping Q3 a separate bounded task.

**Q4 / H4 independent public caption:** use one stage-fixed, safely located PublicCardAnnouncement band, sourced only from up-to-date proven public checkpoints. It may change copy as response windows genuinely advance; it must not change its position because a card shrinks, parks or a timer ticks. Retire old-stage copy as soon as authoritative semantic scope changes (e.g. no leftover "Negation can be played" during Group response); do not reveal private submissions. Measure the actual newly deployed H4 implementation, accept existing H3 font/copy, and preserve USER-ACCEPTED U13-B activity chip. H4 centre deviation ≤12px and vertical movement ≤16px apply under unchanged safe geometry; avoid top Seats, current central/parked physical cards, timer, Local Dock and chip.

**Q1 instrumentation additions:** privacy-reviewed diagnostic trace should log paired PROVEN server and presenter IDs and this public-only record per render checkpoint: build SHA, session ID, monotonic elapsed time, room/interaction/frame/checkpoint/revision, root event/card/author, current participant, current viewer/layout epoch, rendererKind/motionType/visualPhase, reveal/move start and elapsed, number + DOM path/rect of every SAME-root full/parked instance, last viewer rect versus current anchor rect, H4 notice event/semantic stage/text key + rect, safe stage/obstacle rects. Redact all private Hand identities, other players' Negation availability/intents, credentials, secret roles. Instrument ahead of behavior changes and avoid treating presentationRevision as a new physical-card identity.

**Deterministic acceptance invariants across each sampled paint:**

1. Exactly one real, newly committed root reveals centrally, moves to proven author once, then remains PARKED during all later target responses; zero old-root central full-size instances after PARKED. New separately committed physical response is allowed centrally.
2. Root presenter retains constant physical ID/author under all server checkpoints and up to final proven retirement; renderer arbitration prevents both a live physical Journey and an independent same-card static fallback.
3. Post-switch PARKED card's **first painted** x/y is its newly measured actual-author anchor (≤2px rounding tolerance), not any interpolated point from previous-view x/y; no re-reveal/clock reset.
4. Exactly one current public narration for a given stage, with current proven copy, H4-safe geometry and no collision with accepted U13-B chip, HP, countdown, Seat histories or Local Hand.
5. Server rules, CurrentAction legal controls, full physical conservation, Policy-B timing, multi-seat Negation Plan A privacy, R1/R2/Da Qiao/Lust and existing accepted UX remain unchanged.

**Mandatory proof:** run a real authenticated 4-client Worker/D1 Barbarian Invasion and Raining Arrows through Negation, 3 sequential participant responses, damage/trigger and final settlement; match the EXACT root/card/author IDs across clients (arrival times need not match exactly). Separately stress TEST SWITCH 1→2→3→4→1, both during initial reveal and after parking; reconnect/late join, slow polls, viewport 390×700, 390×844, captured 440×766, 440×894 and 480×900, both 4-seat top row and 6/8/10 side-seat topology, reduced motion. Capture DOM states/frame rects/caption history rather than rely on smoke or still screenshots alone. Do not confuse a single TEST SWITCH browser with four real clients. Exact-SHA CI/Worker version and tests must be actually observed; if Q1's current explicit ban on local automated tests remains, honour it and label unverified claims. Technical delivery is PENDING HUMAN REVIEW.

**Sequence and task ownership:** Current HANDOVER.md Q1 is still IN PROGRESS, with diagnostic changes reportedly local-only. **Do not reset, overwrite or begin Q2 in place of that uncommitted work.** Q1 → Q2 → Q3 → Q4 are four individually bounded Coding Agent tasks; finish one and then replace the handoff with the next. The first thing Agent must do on resumption is re-read this latest design revision and preserve its local Q1 instrumentation. No implementation, post-fix game verification, new H4 failure or user visual acceptance is claimed by this document revision.


#### 10.15.6 Executable three-surface presentation specification — MUST / MUST NOT (2026-10-17 clarification)

**P0 user requirement in one sentence:** For the **same proven real card**, the (A) central full-sized card, (B) returned small author-attached card and (C) shared public explanatory text must describe one coherent public event across time and viewpoints; they must not independently restart, move between owners, disagree, or resurrect old stages. This section is **normative over cosmetic placement preferences** and can be handed directly to the Coding Agent. The problem is **presentation arbitration and rehydration, not changes to the WTK rules or CurrentAction**.

**Three surfaces have distinct jobs and must NEVER compete for authority:**

| Surface | When it exists | Where it belongs | Exactly what it may display | What it must NEVER do |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **A. Centre REVEAL / MOVE CardFace** | Only for a **newly committed, server-proven physical card** whose event has not already completed its current viewer's reveal/move lifecycle; newer committed cards may interrupt | Central battlefield safe reveal area, then animate toward true author | ONE current full-size real card with real card ID, owner and event ID | Rerender an old parked root because the group advances to the next target; invent a second root from settlement; run again after TEST SWITCH/poll/refresh |
| **B. PARKED miniature/history** | The same physical event after MOVE, or immediately on viewing already-committed history after switching/late joining/reconnecting | Actual **author's** Seat or own Dock in this observer's current topology (global chronological ordering retained) | The same real physical card, shrunk once, with genuine ordinal when applicable | Fly from a previous observer's seat; change owner; grow back into a centre card; disappear merely because next participant must answer |
| **C. Public event narration (H4)** | While there is a **current, proven public stage/event** requiring a shared explanation; update or retire as proven semantic stage changes | Its **own fixed central safe reading band** (not a child of the card's position/transform) | Current proven actor, action, target, response or outcome text; accepted H3 typography/copy | Follow A or B; lag with a prior Negation/target message; duplicate a separate generic toast; overlap A/B, player HP, response countdown, Dock or accepted U13-B status plaque |

**Strict state-transition contract for one root:**

~~~text
PROVEN NEW ROOT [event/card/author] -> REVEAL(max 4000ms by Policy B)
                                  -> MOVE(about 380ms, to THIS viewer's true-author anchor)
                                  -> PARKED (same real card; stay parked through all target decisions)
                                  -> RETIRED (only when proven history-display policy retires it)

PARKED + target-2/3 response = PARKED (no large re-display)
PARKED + GROUP_RESOLVED      = PARKED (no new static large card)
PARKED + presentationRevision/checkpoint changes = PARKED
PARKED + TEST SWITCH / resize = PARKED at recalculated destination, SNAP WITHOUT TRANSITION
PARKED + poll / reconnect / remount = PARKED, never replay by default
NEW different committed response card = its OWN new centre lifecycle; prior root stays PARKED

C: Proven public semantic stage N -> stage N+1 = replace/retire stage-N text;
   C position remains in its safe band and is not coupled to A/B transforms.
~~~

**Timing and interruption:** The intended REVEAL hold is **up to approximately 4 seconds**, and MOVE about **380ms**, not an unconditional 4-second blocking delay. A newer, *genuinely committed and publicly proven* response may interrupt the prior centre reveal; no purely local response intent or transient UI checkpoint may do so. No new card? Then no fresh REVEAL. A network poll, target index advance, damage/skill settlement, stage revision or visual viewport change is **not** a new physical commitment. Reduced-motion users see equivalent identity/placement without forced flight. Do not infer timing/ownership from a DOM timer.

**Cross-player contract using the recorded genuine Barbarian root:**

| State / viewer | Expected A centre | Expected B miniature | Expected C narration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Viewer 0 first sees root 76af7aee… newly committed | One initial REVEAL, then MOVE | Same root parks under actual source 72383b7b… | Initial **server-proven** public root/action message; H4 safe band |
| Viewer 0 watches target 2, then target 3 respond | **No second root** at centre (new genuine response CardFaces only if proved) | Root d7478792… remains under that same author | Text advances to the newly proven response/target; previous text removed |
| TEST SWITCH viewer 0→1→2→3 during group response | **No root REVEAL restart** | Same physical root **snaps directly** to each new viewer's measured author Seat/Dock, zero flight from prior layout | Current public semantic message reprojects into the viewer's **own safe H4 band**, does not trail the card |
| Group settles | **No 88×128px old-root static fallback** | Root history remains on true author until normal proven retirement | Only current group settlement outcome; obsolete response/Negation message gone |
| Newly connected independent client after root was already public | **No historical replay as fresh commitment** | Reconstructed parked root in the actual author slot according to the existing history policy | Latest proven public stage, not delayed or cached private copy |

**Renderer exclusivity is a functional requirement, not a styling tweak:** At any observation of a given physical root, the system must have a **single effective visual owner** (one typed Journey rather than Journey competing with static-fallback). No full-size static root CardFace is permitted once that root is PARKED, even if a Group settlement produces a different action object; no CSS-only hiding of a duplicate which is still independently driving stale notice state. Display identity must use proven interaction/root/card/author linkage and **must not depend on** checkpointId, presentationRevision, current target seat, current player name, current viewerId or DOM coordinates. Those fields can update **semantics** or **layout**, not reset the root's visual clock.

**Presentation and geometry must be separated:** The root's logical phase/event history survives updates. On a viewerId/Seat topology/visualViewport change, invalidate old coordinates, measure new DOM anchors, and suppress transitions **before painting** any already parked historical card. A client may have one viewer-relative position per layout epoch, but may NEVER interpolate from previous-view x=338px to new-view x=196px for an already PARKED card. If current true-author anchor is unavailable, wait/withhold rendering; never guess location/owner. Same rule applies to legacy Attack/Dodge, Negation, Duel/Lust and other real physical card Journeys, without expanding Q2 beyond Group roots.

**Public narration freshness:** The H4 text's semantic event ID/checkpoint must track current **PROVEN** server interaction state. At confirmed advance, replace the prior stage's visible copy atomically: e.g. “Waiting for Negation” cannot linger when response ownership has advanced to a proven group target; “target 1 responded” cannot still be the visible current instruction while target 2 is acting. The narration's placement is determined by a safe stage band; it must not be recalculated from A's shrinking card or B's parked anchor. When geometry changes (e.g. viewer switch) the safe band may legitimately reproject, but under **unchanged layout** its centre drifts no more than 12px horizontally and 16px vertically. **NEW REQUIRED UX BEHAVIOR — all active in-game selection/progress windows:**
1. **SWITCH ALWAYS WORKS:** An authorised TEST controller must be able to tap every eligible top/side Seat SWITCH **while a choice window is present**, immediately display the switched viewer's **server-authorised** CurrentAction and private Hand, and continue the exact same public root/Harvest selection. Other viewers may INSPECT and SWITCH but never use hidden/private selection options. 4. **Click regions must be surgical:** Presentation/backdrop is \`pointer-events:none\`; the exact bounded interactive **decision panel + its legitimate controls** is \`pointer-events:auto\` **only where actor-authorised**. Passive Harvest spectator progress (message, public available/taken cards) must not capture clicks; pointer-safe public cards may optionally be separately inspectable if truly public, but do not expose/treat them as selectable by a spectator. No whole-stage or whole-viewport transparent hitbox, \`inert\` on game shell, body scroll lock or keyboard focus trap. A perspective switch during an in-flight optimistic Harvest preview/choice must not copy its private draft into the next actor's selection or wrongly mark a card taken. Reset **viewer-local unsent selection only**, not authoritative committed choices; preserve legitimate actions in the current viewer even while inspecting unrelated objects.
6. **Mobile UI/focus:** On 390×700, 390×844, 440×766 (uploaded), 440×894, 480×900 and 6/8/10 seats, panel stays within visible safe area without physically covering SWITCH/critical Dock/Inspector targets, and can scroll internally when crowded. Keyboard/VoiceOver can reach both dialog controls and independent board actions; remove inaccurate \`aria-modal="true"\`, maintain accurate accessible decision-owner/status labels, focus after SWITCH and no inaccessible "trap" or accidental choice.
7. **Scope is all comparable active-game selection interfaces, not marketing/site-wide dialogs:** Bumper Harvest first; public-target card choice for **Steal and Burning Bridges**; shared \`TargetCardPicker\` for skills/equipment (including Frost Sword, Kirin Bow and Retaliation) and \`MandatoryChoiceDialog\`; private Stargazing reorder; private Legacy distribution. Also inspect \`weapon-response-backdrop\`, \`table-hidden-card-picker\` and any *current* card selection overlays for equivalent hit interception. An unrelated page history, login/exit confirmation or pre-game modal is **not automatically** in scope without proof that it blocks an active match.

For every picker, **re-tap a selected card to deselect**. A separate **Cancel** is exceptionally rare and NOT part of default UX; do not automatically replace it with a new Clear button. #### 10.17.0 BCP-0 — common selection UX contract (ALL scoped extra-choice dialogs)

- **Physical layout:** A compact bordered dark/gold floating component positioned INSIDE the measured battle/interaction-safe region, below actual opponent seats, above the Local Dock/Hand/Guidance and outside the timer and accepted H4 public narration. No viewport-sized opaque sheet, no global touch overlay, no click intercept outside bounded controls. At 390×700 and 440×766 the footer must be visible at first view without page scrolling; large content is scrollable INSIDE the card area, preferably horizontal rows. Header one short skill/effect title + one instruction, minimal extra filler. Use consistent card aspect ratios and scales (may be smaller than ordinary hand cards, still legible through Details), close spacing and responsive safe geometry.
- **Selection:** Use server-given eligible card/opaque position keys, min/max, target, and current decision actor. Gold outline + check + modest raised position for selected. First tap adds, **repeat tap same key removes**; a different tap changes one-card choice, multi-choice selection toggles individually. Limit selection according to authoritative min/max. Selected count concise (e.g. `1 / 2`). **Confirm disabled until exactly server-permitted amount**, submits once under action revision guard. Tapping Info does NOT toggle/select/submit. No client-generated extra hidden Hand keys.
- **Buttons:** Default mandatory decision footer = **Confirm ONLY**, plus tap-to-deselect. Optional server-permitted reaction footer = **Use/Confirm + Skip ONLY**, Skip calls the exact authoritative decline action; do not show Cancel. **Do not add Clear/Reset by default.** An independently real, legitimate *abort of the whole uncommitted flow* may exceptionally use Cancel only after the Agent identifies the specific legal semantics and seeks product owner approval; do not equate Cancel with deselect or Skip. Existing internal onCancel function may remain as draft cleanup for viewer/session change, but should not be rendered as a redundant visible button. A genuinely complex drag/reorder-only Reset requires separate proof, not automatic generalization.
- **Safe viewing/privacy:** Spectators may SWITCH/Inspect real public Heroes, their own Hand Details and public Equipment where allowed, but must not gain an unauthorized selection or see hidden identities. Hand backs remain anonymous with existing `hand:n` or single random-hand server key. Information icons appear only on genuinely public identifiable choice cards and are wholly inside the card edge, accessible by touch and keyboard. Info view can close without dismissing pending choice. Respect current session/actor when switching TEST viewers: do not leak or submit stale drafts; preserve server deadlines/confirmed picks.
- **Different content, consistent frame:** Standard 1-card target picker (Steal/Burning Bridges), multi-card skill picker (Frost Sword), revealed public pool (Harvest), assignment (Legacy), rearrangement (Stargazing) may use different inner controls but the SAME title/card/footer vocabulary and bounded nonmodal geometry. Keep existing straightforward local Equipment-cost selection in Local Dock instead of forcing unnecessary duplicate popup. Actual per-effect rules and W5/W6 public effect Frame review remain distinct.

The separately parked root at lower-left has a **disproportionately large suit/rank corner** compared to its actual thumbnail. **Normative phone design:**
1. **Measured content-fit width, NOT viewport/battlefield 94–100% width by default:** size frame to visible actual public cards + authentic card gaps + small padding + necessary footer; center the compact frame within the battlefield's protected safe region. For 4 public cards at 390/440px, increase individual real card face size versus IMG_3304 while still fitting those four cards and their Info hit regions; allow modest card count-dependent width up to available battlefield width. Use a comfortable small visible glyph and ≥44×44px effective touch target where geometry permits (do not cover neighboring choice cards); maintain readable suit/rank and card art/title, no ambiguous overlap with select/tap target. Distinct event propagation: Info opens the correct existing Card Details nonmodally without toggling, selecting, unselecting, confirming or dismissing the pool. Selecting card body toggles, re-tapping same body deselects per §10.17.0. The observer's public cards may also open safe Info but no spectator can select or Confirm.

Never alter the physical card's suit/rank, card ID, genuine owner Seat, public ordinal or original reveal dimensions. Follow the root CardFace from centre reveal → MOVE → PARKED and inspect computed CSS: code candidates include \`app/globals.css\` \`.public-card-journey-card[data-public-card-journey-size="thumbnail"] > .played-card > i\` (plus \`i small\`, card name) and higher-specificity \`app/sequence-overrides.css\` / the actual rendered layer. **Do not assume a single CSS rule is the applied winner:** measure computed styles/screenshots for the actual thumbnail and compare its corner-to-card size ratio with the same card's reveal.
4. **Footer and nonblocking guarantees:** An authorized active chooser keeps one clearly visible \`Confirm choice\` in compact panel footer, disabled until exactly one genuine available card is selected, no standalone Cancel/Clear. The footer is visible without *page* scroll at iPhone 390×700; private selection and pending action are preserved across an Info toggle. #### 10.17.1.2 BCP-1-F2 — KEEP a safe read-only Harvest card pool during the NEGATION response window (2026-10-18 USER NOT ACCEPTED; PUBLIC-PROOF AUDIT BEFORE CODE)

**Observed behavior and product requirement:** IMG_3305.jpeg shows a real Bumper Harvest root parked under its author, the H4 message \`JD played Bumper Harvest; Negation can be played.\`, and an activity chip \`Waiting for Negation responses\` with ~5s remaining; **the Bumper Harvest public choice cards are no longer on screen**. Product owner explicitly wants the full applicable pool visible for visual planning during Negation, but in **read-only/no-selection mode**; Negation awaiting is not a reason to erase already genuinely public information. Do not interpret this as permission to prematurely reveal unrevealed deck cards.

**State and authority investigation (mandatory):**
1. In current \`app/page.tsx\`, the pool's existing mount predicate is \`room.pendingHarvest && !presentationBusy\`, while \`canChooseHarvest\` also requires a response currentAction. The screenshot's Negation wait does not by itself prove \`room.pendingHarvest\` existed; the server may not have generated or publicly projected the pool *until after* the Negation window. Instrument/replay exact timeline from root COMMIT → Negation offered → one or more Negation responses → no Negation / countered Negation / accepted Negation → initial pool reveal and each choice. **A. Root-cause assessment, from current \`ux-v2\` source (separate certainty levels):**

- **CONFIRMED structural width defect:** In \`app/page.tsx\`, \`OpponentPublicInspector\` renders \`<div className="hero-focus-body">…</div>\` followed by the SIBLING \`<div className="hero-focus-inspect-details hero-focus-context">…</div>\`. The last effective general rule in \`app/sequence-overrides.css\` at the end of the file constrains that sibling to \`width: calc(4 * var(--inspector-slot-width) + 8px)\` (approximately **152 CSS px** for the four 36px top-row slots). But the complete panel is wider because it ALSO includes the portrait (roughly 74–80px on a phone) and a small inter-column gap. **This explicitly prevents the Entourage and Serpent Spear explanations from spanning the complete Inspector.** The screenshots exhibit exactly this narrow block. This is the first required fix, not a typography problem.
- **CODE-SUPPORTED width-allocation risk, not independently measured root cause:** \`.opponent-inspection-floating-shell\`, its inner \`.hero-focus.hero-focus-inspect\`, \`.hero-focus-body\`, and \`.hero-focus-inspect-main\` all use \`width: max-content\`, with the body's right track \`minmax(0, max-content)\`. The equipment row independently fixes four slot widths plus gaps. Together these intrinsic sizes can create an unbalanced two-column frame: the right-hand content crowds its left edge while some trailing horizontal space remains. \`.hero-focus-inspect-upper\` also has a 72px minimum for identity and a \`max-content\` Judgement column. **Confirm which computed box actually owns the gutter** using DOMRects before changing one or more of these constraints; do not assume the percentage of whitespace from screenshot pixels equals measured CSS px.
- **CODE-SUPPORTED short-height scrolling risk:** There are conflicting earlier declarations in the SAME CSS file: \`.hero-focus-inspect-details\` was \`overflow:auto\` near the original Inspector rules, then \`overflow:visible\` in the INS-5 pass, while the compact rules cap skill text to about five lines; the card-detail grid has \`overflow:hidden\` with scrolling on the actual rules paragraph. The floating shell gets a dynamic safe \`maxHeight\` from \`app/page.tsx\` \`OpponentPublicInspector\` \`useLayoutEffect\` and the Inspector itself may overflow. The iPhone captures show text running toward the bottom; **whether the last line is actually inaccessible must be verified**, not guessed. Ensure a complete functional scroll chain within the safe vertical height.

**C. Objective acceptance contract — all must be measured, not inferred from element existence:**

- **Four-seat geometry (390×700 and 390×844):** the entire right main/HP/skill/Judgement/Equipment region starts **at least 2 CSS px to the RIGHT of the left portrait column** and never overlaps the image or caption. Exactly 2 Judgement + 4 Equipment slots still have ~1–3 CSS px inter-slot gaps and their card rank/suit artwork remains readable. The rightmost used equipment slot/section should be within **8 CSS px** of the Inspector's usable content-right (excluding documented border/padding), or the Agent reports measured why the remaining gap is necessary; an empty ~10–20px+ internal band is not acceptable. No horizontal page overflow, no masked content; the outer shell stays inside the existing player-seat-free corridor, approximately the existing compact footprint (existing four-seat browser expectation ≤262 CSS px unless impossible with preserved readable content).
- **Detail width, BOTH types:** in expanded Entourage and Serpent Spear state, \`DetailsHost.width\` should be within **4 CSS px** of the measured Inspector inner content width; its skill/card text background should extend across at least **90% of that usable width** and finish within the right border with at most ordinary padding. It must be **materially wider than the previous fixed four-slot width**, not another paragraph positioned on the left with a blank right strip. Actual rendered wrapped lines must fit without horizontal overflow, last-line text loss, or overlapping the public chip. The empty, unexpanded Details state does not leave a tall blank zone.
- **Vertical readability:** test scrolling to the FINAL sentence of a long skill and a long card description, including short-height 390×700 and 440×766. Assert via scrollTop/scrollHeight/clientHeight **AND** final text DOM Range visibility at the bottom of the scrollport after scrolling, not just \`toContainText\` or a mounted paragraph. Inspector safe bottom stays above activity chip, System Menu / Guidance and Hand/Dock.
- **Responsive / real state:** \`390×700\`, \`390×844\`, \`440×766\`, \`440×894\`, \`480×900\` plus 4/6/8/10-seat when fixture-supported; empty/1/4 Equipment, 0/2/3+ Judgement, 0/1/2+ skills, Chinese/English long Hero and player captions. All genuine visible public card suit/rank/identity remain true; hidden Hand and roles remain hidden. Long labels cannot cause new body overflow or an inaccessible button.
- **Interaction safety:** same Hero click closes; a different Hero switches; selecting/returning from public Equipment/Judgement and switching Skill ↔ Card Details remains consistent; Escape and focus restoration work; a valid current target/Confirm survives inspect open/close; opponent SWITCH remains clickable where authorized; no interference with H4 stable public narration, public activity chip, timer and local Hand.
- **Completion evidence:** one focused commit for the **same INS-5-F1-R** scoped code/tests, before/after screenshots and tabulated DOMRects with both opened Details states on small iPhones, relevant negative tests, exact-SHA CI/build/lint/API/browser results and actual Worker/deploy/smoke. Report \`IMPLEMENTED / DEPLOYED — PENDING USER PHONE RE-REVIEW\` only after proof. Never mark this design **USER ACCEPTED** without the owner's next phone verdict.

**D. Stop conditions:** If preserving four slots + portrait while removing overlap cannot fit a particular 6/8/10-seat phone corridor, do NOT hide a slot, shrink real cards beyond readability, alter seat positions or inject private gameplay state. Stop that unsupported geometry, record exact DOMRects/screenshots and request the minimal UX owner choice. Keep the already working 4-seat path and other authorized task changes safe. These instructions implement the owner's existing design; the Agent should not invent a new visual scheme.

## 11. UX3 FUTURE backlog — Negation Plan B, immediate-public-on-Confirm (NOT AN UX2 TASK)

**User preference recorded 2026-10-14 (future UX3 only):** Explore individual immediate-public Negation Confirm through a separately approved future UX3 design. This is NOT authorized autonomous UX2 work. UX2 Negation Plan A remains private all-seat submission, maximum 8s and early server settlement only after EVERY living seat explicitly responds.

**Non-trivial unresolved tradeoff:** UX2 private simultaneous intents still use canonical seat priority; all-seats explicit early completion doesn't change that. Immediately displaying an INDIVIDUAL's Confirm as a PUBLIC card, while others remain undecided, could expose a losing intent, consume a wrong card or leak secret availability. UX3 requires a separately approved authoritative arbitration rule, not an optimistic animation or simply changing the 8s maximum.

**UX3 questions to design with user before authorization:** (1) first-arrival commitment versus stable clockwise seat priority / override grace window; (2) protecting opponent Negation availability and private Hand when no card is submitted; (3) new counter windows, timeouts, fairness under network lag and reconnect; (4) atomic exactly-once actual card consumption, conflicting simultaneous confirms, Duel/Group/Judgement consistency; (5) visible chronological author-attached card history, animation interruption, old-save migration, rollbacks and authenticated multi-viewer privacy tests.

**Separate UX3 design decision, not an UX2 review gate:** UX3 needs distinct product/rules authorization because it changes priority, privacy and fairness. Continue all approved UX2 work without awaiting acceptance of prior UX2 items, but do not silently implement this unapproved UX3 rule.
